> [!TIP]

## About this list

This list is kept by [Wobo](https://www.wobo.ai/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=list&utm_campaign=internships&utm_content=footer) and built from the roles Wobo reads on employers' own career sites, never from other job boards. It is rebuilt every hour from the employer sites Wobo checks every day, and a role leaves the list when its employer closes it.

- Tech here means software, AI and ML, data, security, product and design, and hardware. Hardware also covers electrical, mechanical, manufacturing and process engineering.
- Dates are the employer's posting date. When an employer gives none, the role is dated by the day it was first found. Dates without a year are in 2026.
- Pay is shown where the employer lists it. Visa sponsorship is not marked; check the job page.
- [Report a closed or wrong role](https://github.com/Wobo-ai/Summer2027-Internships/issues/new?template=report-a-role.yml) with the link of the role.
- [Suggest a missing company](https://github.com/Wobo-ai/Summer2027-Internships/issues/new?template=suggest-a-company.yml) with its careers page.
- The list is generated, so pull requests are not merged.
- Internships in every field, not only tech: Intern Job List at [internjoblist.com](https://www.internjoblist.com/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=list&utm_campaign=internships&utm_content=footer).
- More Wobo lists on GitHub: [New Grad Tech Jobs](https://github.com/Wobo-ai/New-Grad-Jobs) and [Remote Tech Jobs (US)](https://github.com/Wobo-ai/Remote-Tech-Jobs).
